INSERT INTO parse_groups_insert (upload_group,description,is_active,db_id) VALUES
('dexa','This is a DEXA scan. A DEXA scan is a medical imaging test most commonly used to:

Measure body composition
- Fat mass
- Lean (muscle) mass
- Bone mass

Assess bone health
- Detect osteopenia or osteoporosis
- Fat loss / muscle gain
- Bone density change

Main Sections You’ll See in a DEXA Report

1. Body Composition
This section breaks your body into components.

Common values:
- Total Mass – Your total body weight measured by the scan
- Fat Mass (lbs or kg) – Total body fat weight
- Lean Mass (lbs or kg) – Everything that isn’t fat or bone (mostly muscle)
- Bone Mineral Content (BMC) – Actual bone mass

Key percentage:
Body Fat %

Men:
- Athletic: ~6–13%
- Average: ~14–24%
- High: 25%+

Women:
- Athletic: ~14–20%
- Average: ~21–31%
- High: 32%+

2. Regional Breakdown
DEXA doesn’t just give totals — it breaks the body into regions:
- Arms
- Legs
- Trunk (chest + abdomen)
- Android (abdominal fat)
- Gynoid (hips/thighs)

Why this matters:
- Shows fat distribution, not just how much fat you have
- Central (abdominal) fat is more metabolically risky than hip/leg fat

3. Visceral Adipose Tissue (VAT)
This is one of the most important metrics.

VAT (lbs or cm²) = fat surrounding internal organs

Strongly linked to:
- Heart disease
- Diabetes
- Metabolic syndrome

General interpretation (varies by lab):
- Low: very healthy
- Moderate: acceptable
- High: increased health risk

4. Lean Mass & Muscle Balance
Often shown per limb:
- Right arm vs left arm
- Right leg vs left leg

This helps identify:
- Muscle imbalances
- Injury-related atrophy
- Training asymmetry

5. Bone Density (if included)
Values you may see:
- BMD (Bone Mineral Density)
- T-score
  - Normal: ≥ -1.0
  - Osteopenia: -1.0 to -2.5
  - Osteoporosis: ≤ -2.5
- Z-score (age-adjusted comparison)

What This Report Is Commonly Used For:
- Fitness & body recomposition tracking
- Medical risk assessment
- Hormonal or metabolic evaluation
- Baseline before weight loss, bulking, or rehab
- Bone health screening','1','b29c76d2-8f4b-43bc-ac4e-fdfafb9674fe')
